review_text: WORTH A VISIT
We went on a Monday at 1pm, it was pretty slow, just a couple of other people dining, and the employees were a basic crew. We walked in and had just enough time to look at the menu and really decide what we wanted since it was our first time without being rushed by the staff. I got the BBQ Nachos, my husband got a turkey sandwich, I noticed he got 2 sides and I think for the $15 price that's soooo worth it. My husband said the Mac and Cheese was wonderful, the sweet potato fries weren't anything special, but he enjoyed them. The bbq nachos were some of the best I've had. The restaurant is very clean, and decorated well. Also there is an awesome mural on the side of the building.

review_text: We stopped at Hog Wild Beach BBQ and enjoyed a nice change of pace from all the seafood. We ordered the Tantrum, the Nasty G, and the kids grilled cheese. entity_slug: hog-wild-beach-bbq
reviewer_name: Emily Tyo
rating: 5
body: WORTH A VISIT
We went on a Monday at 1pm, it was pretty slow, just a couple of other people dining, and the employees were a basic crew. We walked in and had just enough time to look at the menu and really decide what we wanted since it was our first time without being rushed by the staff. I got the BBQ Nachos, my husband got a turkey sandwich, I noticed he got 2 sides and I think for the $15 price that's soooo worth it. My husband said the Mac and Cheese was wonderful, the sweet potato fries weren't anything special, but he enjoyed them. The bbq nachos were some of the best I've had. The restaurant is very clean, and decorated well. Also there is an awesome mural on the side of the building.

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: b996075d-edf9-463b-814a-e8a752691e91
entity_slug: hog-wild-beach-bbq
reviewer_name: Russell Wright
rating: 5
body: We stopped at Hog Wild Beach BBQ and enjoyed a nice change of pace from all the seafood. We ordered the Tantrum, the Nasty G, and the kids grilled cheese. I dont know why but I also loved the fries they served. While it may not be the best BBQ in the state, it was definitely flavorful and satisfying. I recommend the bourbon  flavored BBQ sauce.

The BBQ is served dry, which we appreciated since it lets you choose your own sauce to suit your taste. The meat was tender and well cooked, and the portion sizes were generous.

As with our other dining experiences on this trip, the service was excellent. Friendly staff, quick food, and a relaxed atmosphere made this a pleasant stop. We’ll definitely consider coming back next time we're in town.
